## Ann Arbor

*Dear Śrīla Prabhupāda.*

Please accept our most humble obeisances in the most sacred dust of your lotus feet. Each year it is a great honor to be able to offer you our attempted praises on the most auspicious day of your appearance, Śrī Vyāsa-pūjā. However, because you are so great a pure devotee, these sincere statements and feelings will always be inadequate and imperfect.

*durlabho mānuṣo deho*  
*dehināṁ kṣaṇa-bhaṅguraḥ*  
*tatrāpi durlabhaṁ manye*  
*vaikuṇṭha-priya-darśanam*

**"For the conditioned souls, the human body is most difficult to achieve, and it can be lost at any moment. But I think that even those who have achieved human life rarely gain the association of pure devotees, who are dear to the Lord of Vaikuṇṭha."** ***(Śrīmad-Bhāgavatam*** **11.2.29)**

Needless to say, our lives were useless and miserable before meeting you, Śrīla Prabhupāda. Like a dog chasing his tail, we were vainly running after the objects of the senses, never finding the end or satisfaction. Being kept in ignorance and illusion by modern politicians, scientists, teachers, and even parents and friends, we had no idea that the supreme goal of life was to elevate ourselves in Kṛṣṇa consciousness and render pure devotional service to Lord Kṛṣṇa, the Supreme Personality of Godhead and reservoir of all pleasure.

This is your great mercy, Śrīla Prabhupāda, that you are distributing pure love of God all over this materially ravaged world, administering the medicine to cure all the ills of Kali-yuga. The *Bhāgavatam* states,

*tasmād guruṁ prapadyeta*  
*jijñāsuḥ śreya uttamam*  
*śābde pare ca niṣṇātaṁ*  
*brahmaṇy upaśamāśrayam*

**"Therefore any person who seriously desires real happiness must seek out a bona fide spiritual master and take shelter of him by initiation. The qualification of the bona** ***fide guru*** **is that he has realized the conclusions of the scriptures by deliberation and is able to convince others of these conclusions. Such great personalities, who have taken shelter of the Supreme Godhead, leaving aside all material considerations, should be understood to be bona fide spiritual masters."** ***(Śrīmad-Bhāgavatam*** **11.3.21)**

We rededicate ourselves, on this most auspicious Vyāsa-pūjā, to your mission of spreading Kṛṣṇa consciousness throughout the world, and we pray for your guidance and shelter. All glories to you, *Jagad-guru* Śrīla Prabhupāda!

*Your fallen servants,*

**Disciples of Śrīla Prabhupāda**

Daśaratha dāsa

**Granddisciples**

Śivānanda Sena dāsa / Bhakta Mike / Bhakta Robert / Bhakta Steve / Bhakta Anthony

## Argentina

*Dear Śrīla Prabhupāda,*

Please accept our humble obeisances at your lotus feet.

Today is once again the auspicious day of your appearance, and all over the world your followers are gathering in front of your *vyāsāsana* to meditate on you and to offer you special worship.

On this day we feel intense separation from you, and we regret very much not having taken the rare moments of your personal association more seriously. You appeared in the darkness of our miserable lives and woke us up to the blissful reality of Kṛṣṇa consciousness. You gave us the holy names, the association of devotees worldwide, your books—the gifts you have given us cannot be counted. Everything we have and everything we are comes from you. We are completely made up of your mercy. Even now, after you have disappeared from our sight, your guidance is still all-pervasive. We are especially grateful to have the guidance of your beloved son, Harikeśa Swami, in whom we see the perfect example of your surrendered disciple.

This day is joyous as well as a sad occasion. On one side we realize our fortunate position to have met you and to be your disciples, but on the other side we lament our inability to come up to your expectations. O Prabhupāda, if we could only give up these useless desires for sense gratification once and for all. Time is running out so fast. Years are lost without our really making any use of them. Please force yourself into our hearts and kick out all the painful *anarthas* that hold us back from surrendering to you. Give us the intelligence and the determination to utilize without fail all opportunities to push on your movement in this hellish place. We want to make your mission our mission, and especially we want to print and distribute more and more books. It is the most effective method to counteract the influence of this horrible age, and we know that this is what pleases you most.

*We beg to remain your humble servants,*

BUENOS AIRES

## Athens, Greece

*Dear Śrīla Prabhupāda,*

Please accept my humble obeisances at your lotus feet, which have graced the earth and the hearts of those you came across. All glories to you on this auspicious day of your appearance!

*manuṣyāṇāṁ sahasreṣu*  
*kaścid yatati siddhaye*  
*yatatām api siddhānāṁ*  
*kaścin māṁ vetti tattvataḥ*

**"Out of many thousands among men, one may endeavor for perfection, and of those who have achieved perfection, hardly one knows Me in truth."**

Out of millions of people, most are fools simply running after what animals attain without extraneous efforts and what countless billions of other fools before them were also eager for. It's all the same story, and millennium after millennium history repeats itself in different guises, for the desire to enjoy is very great, and the senses and the mind are impetuous. Misguided, they think that to gratify these senses is in their self-interest, and so society as a whole is pushed nearer the edge of a precarious precipice.

A few others, because of slightly better intellect, understand the syndrome of ordinary people, and they prefer to set themselves apart by being those who do the cheating instead of those who are cheated. They become controllers and exploit others' desires to enjoy for their own gains. These are our present-day capitalists, who capitalize on others' weaknesses and stupidity.

Still others want fame and adoration, because they have the same deep-rooted desires to be the center, and for that they may also undergo austerities and penances. A fewer still become successful. These are our politicians, scientists, artists, celebrities, and other so-called great men. The more intelligent of these become disillusioned with all this fame, and they turn either to searching for higher values or take shelter of intoxication and become more degraded.

Then there are those who are considered selfless by materialistic men, who for their community, country, or some ideology make personal sacrifices, even sometimes giving up their lives.

Even greater are those who go beyond ideological or political barriers and become benevolent. They appear to make selfless sacrifices for the betterment of humanity under the banners of so many "isms" or some welfare program. But is not this simply extended selfishness, that because one is human one helps other humans? Because of the lack of ultimate knowledge, this help is very limited.

Fewer still know that higher enjoyment is there in the heavens. They understand that there are laws determining one's enjoyment and suffering and that someone is maintaining those laws. They prefer pious acts and sacrifices to appease the demigods, and they follow various scriptural injunctions and for millions of years enjoy heavenly delights.

*Yogīs* cannot wait, and in this life they unleash the mystic powers of the soul, but use them for their own ends.

Sometimes we see those who, tired of material enjoyments, begin to desire for liberation from the suffering that is the ultimate conclusion of material life. Those that succeed on this path are few, and they usually end up being impersonalists. Advancement for them is very difficult, says Kṛṣṇa in *Bhagavad-gītā.* They like to speculate and utilize their intelligence to conclude quite rightly that the material world is "not where it's at." Their conclusion is to negate everything, and as a result they end up with nothing. They try to also become nothing in an attempt to stamp out desires, which they appreciate as the cause of entanglement here.

Then there are those who are a little more appreciative of a higher intelligence but who do not credit Him with any qualities. They have tried to become God but have failed and adopt the philosophy of "If you can't beat them, join them!" So they try to become one with Him. This is not possible, and these souls usually end up falling down again in material life. This is the last snare *of māyā,* and most of those who attain liberation fall into her trap.

It seems that the path to the Absolute Truth is thwarted with so many dangers and deviations. Those who are humble enough submit themselves to an authority for spiritual guidance. Only if completely sincere will you find the perfect teacher. Christians also claim submission to a perfect guide, but because most are still full of material desires, they misinterpret scripture to suit their own ends. Others, too, want spiritual life but do not want to make the break with material life. Because this is cheating, they end up being cheated themselves.

Four types of people come to devotional service, and not all are freed from motivation. One who is that sincere seeker is the best. Others may, after a while, once again become diverted, although their service has not gone in vain. Besides, to find someone who has heard from the perfect authority is rare, and rarer still is to find someone who is practicing what he has heard.

Out of those who engage in devotional service, Kṛṣṇa says,

*māṁ ca yo 'vyabhicāreṇa*  
*bhakti-yogena sevate*  
*sa guṇān samatītyaitan*  
*brahma-bhūyāya kalpate*

**"One who engages in full devotional service, who does not fall down in any circumstance, at once transcends the modes of material nature and thus comes to the level of Brahman."**

This position is only realized after many births and deaths:

*bahūnāṁ janmanām ante*  
*jñānavān māṁ prapadyate*  
*vāsudevaḥ sarvam iti*  
*sa mahātmā sudurlabhaḥ*

**"After many births and deaths, he who is actually in knowledge surrenders unto Me, knowing Me to be the cause of all causes and all that is. Such a great soul is very rare."**

By being steady in devotional service one develops a taste, and then one comes to the level of *bhāva,* and the mature stage of *bhava* is called *prema,* or pure love of Godhead. That is the perfect stage of devotional service. Still, amongst those who are perfect, there are those who do not preach. And amongst those who preach, some do not practice. To find someone who preaches and practices is even more uncommon.

So to meet someone like you, Śrīla Prabhupāda, is uncommon indeed. An example of how rare it is, is given in scripture. It is compared to a tortoise swimming at random in the world's vast ocean. On that same ocean somewhere is a plank of wood with a hole only big enough for the head of the tortoise to fit through. The tortoise needs to come up for air only infrequently. To come in contact with a person as qualified as you, Śrīla Prabhupāda, is as unlikely as for the tortoise, when coming up for air, to pass its head through that hole in the plank of wood.

So we find ourselves as fortunate and as grateful as the sages of Naimiṣāraṇya when they met Sūta Gosvāmī. Therefore we consider their prayers as applicable to you as they were to him. The sages said, "You are completely free from all vice. You are well versed in all the scriptures famous for religious life, and in the *Purāṇas* and the histories as well, for you have gone through them under proper guidance and have also explained them.

"And because you are submissive, your spiritual master has endowed you with all the favors bestowed upon a gentle disciple.

"Those who have completely taken shelter of the lotus feet of the Lord can at once sanctify those who come in touch with them, whereas the waters of the Ganges can sanctify only after prolonged use.

"We think that we have met Your Goodness by the will of providence, just so that we may accept you as captain of the ship for those who desire to cross the difficult ocean of Kali, which deteriorates all the good qualities of a human being."

You are the topmost personality in this universe, because you are the perfect representative of the topmost person. That is because your service is *ahaituky apratihatā—*unmotivated and uninterrupted. Even in our line of disciplic succession, no one stands out greater than you, not even Lord Caitanya Mahāprabhu, as this movement never went beyond the shores of Bhārata-varṣa. This is because Kṛṣṇa likes to do things through His pure representatives, so that they can be rightly glorified.

We must conclude that the greatest achievements have gone to you, Śrīla Prabhupāda, and that therefore you must be the dearest devotee of the Lord. Please never allow us to forget your position, and especially our great fortune in having been inconceivably blessed by having come in contact with you.

*Your unworthy disciples and granddisciples in Athens,*

## Athens, Ohio

*Dear Śrīla Prabhupāda,*

Please accept our humble obeisances. When Prahlada Mahārāja saw the Supreme Personality of Godhead, he immediately remembered his spiritual master and felt very indebted to him.

We have no devotion like Prahlada, but we see Kṛṣṇa in another way. We see people changing and taking up Kṛṣṇa consciousness, and we know that Kṛṣṇa is acting to fulfill your desire to save the suffering *karmīs.*

We know you could have easily promoted everyone back to the spiritual world during your pastimes on this planet, but rascals like us would have refused to go. You are so kind to keep us in your service so one day after being fully purified we may join again the "Prabhupāda-līlā."

For us the personified mercy of yourself and Caitanya Mahāprabhu is now evident in the form of Kīrtanānanda Swami. Thank you for the second chance.

*Your disciples,*

Kṛṣṇa-kathā dāsa / Sumati-devī dāsī

**A song for Prabhupāda**

Some people sell out for money  
Some people sell out for fame  
Some leave out of indifference  
They can't accept the blame

When will I see you again?  
When will my life begin again?

Some people have no sense of value  
Some people have no sense of worth  
They throw away the best thing  
Birth after birth after birth

When will I see you again?  
When will my life begin again?  
When will the sunrise  
Reopen these tired eyes?  
You are like moonlight  
To soothe this mind  
In its constant flight

Some people sell out for self-indulgence  
Some people blame it all on fate  
Some people have no patience  
They say they just can't wait

When will I see you again?  
When will my life begin again?

With your heart big as Vaikuṇṭha  
Your smile the spiritual sky  
Your sparkling eyes like cintāmaṇi  
Your love will never die

When will I see you again?  
When will my life begin again?  
Your humble servants in Atlanta.

## Auckland

Śrīla Prabhupāda was born in a holy place  
In the house of his Vaiṣṇava father, by Lord Śrī Kṛṣṇa's grace.

While growing up he pulled his Rathayātrā cart  
All around the neighborhood to gladden his / other's heart.

The future was unknown then—still to unfold—  
Of the unparalleled preacher forceful and bold.

He would take up Bhaktisiddhānta 's mission to deliver the human race  
To the lotus feet of Lord Caitanya, the eternal resting place.

He would travel to America, the land of mleccha-deśa,  
And teach them all to chant and dance, eat gulabjamans, and even make sandeśa.

Chant and dance, take prasādam, this will cure your lust;  
Have faith in devotional service; in the spiritual master trust.

Give up your asslike existence, working day and night;  
Reject the life of cats and dogs, never knowing wrong from right.

Hear from the Bhagavad-gītā of eternal, blissful life;  
No birth or death for the eternal soul, existence free from strife.

Presenting the Vedic conclusions practical and sublime,  
Your books are for the next ten thousand years and on till the end of time.

You have given us everything, nothing else remains—  
Just fill in the framework, no need for fertile brains.

Some fools and great offenders say you preached only to the mass  
And that other "more enlightened" souls preached only to the class.

But what class of men are they that are so spiritually blind  
As not to recognize Śrīla Prabhupāda as the savior of all mankind?

Unfortunately they are bewildered victims of-desire and lust;  
They cannot control their restless minds in which they dearly trust.

"The dogs will bark as the caravan goes past, "you said in all humility.  
We wish the dogs were quiet, but they haven't got the ability.

Your ISKCON caravan is going on and on, relentless in its motion  
To establish Śrīla Prabhupāda as the embodiment of all devotion.

All glories to Śrīla Prabhupāda!  
All glories to Śrīla Prabhupāda!  
All glories to Śrīla Prabhupāda!

*(Written by Jayaśīla dāsa)*

## Baltimore

Please accept my humble obeisances.

By accident you visited here in July 1976, though everyone said you wouldn't be coming. We were told, "There is nothing in Baltimore, so you should all go to Washington, D.C., to see Prabhupāda." However, during an evening *darśana* in Washington, D.C., a devotee gave you a photograph of our beloved Deities, Śrī Śrī Nitāi-Gaurasundara. Upon seeing Their transcendental beauty, you said, "Where are these Deities?"

Rūpānuga: "They are in Baltimore, Śrīla Prabhupāda."

Śrīla Prabhupāda: "So, let us go."

Rūpānuga: "Right now, Prabhupāda?"

Śrīla Prabhupāda: "Yes."

Rūpānuga: "Shouldn't we wait until morning?"

Śrīla Prabhupāda: "So where is this Baltimore? Is it on the way to New York?"

Rūpānuga: "It is just one hour from here, Prabhupāda."

Śrīla Prabhupāda: "Oh, so let us go."

Submitting to your servant's plea, you took rest and came to Baltimore the next morning. You stood motionless before Śrī Śrī Nitāi-Gaurasundara as the *"Govinda"* tape played through its entirety. Before leaving that day, you lectured from *Caitanya-caritāmṛta,* where Sanātana Gosvāmī asked Lord Caitanya, "Who am I?" You also named the Bhaktivedanta Institute journal while talking with Bhaktisvarūpa Dāmodara Mahārāja in the small green cabin behind our temple. You closed your lecture from *Caitanya-caritāmṛta* (*Madhya* 20.102) with these inspiring words:

"That is spiritual salvation, or liberation. If you remain in your spiritual body, then you go back to Godhead. If you go there, you live eternally. This benediction is offered by Lord Caitanya, who has very kindly come here as Nitāi-Gaurasundara. So you take advantage of His mercy. You are very fortunate that Nitāi-Gaura is here. If you simply chant Nitāi-Gaura and dance, then you will become advanced. There is no difficulty. This simple chanting "Jaya Śacīnandana," "Hare Kṛṣṇa," and dancing—*yajñaiḥ saṅkīrtana-prāyaiḥ.* It is not that I am manufacturing it. It is the injunction of the *śāstras.* In this age simply by chanting and dancing you get complete spiritual salvation. So I am very glad you are taking care of Nitāi-Gaura so nicely. They are nicely dressed. So continue these activities, and even if you cannot do anything, simply chant Hare Kṛṣṇa and dance and "Jaya Śacīnandana." That will make your life perfect. Thank you very much."

As you had no intention of stopping here, but were attracted upon seeing a photo of the Deities, similarly I had no desire to engage in spiritual life until I saw you in Los Angeles in 1974. Hearing your explanations of Kṛṣṇa consciousness gave me the first glimpse of my true identity. You revealed that I wasn't a Southern Californian fruitarian-surfer, but an eternal servant of Kṛṣṇa, soon to become a "sufferer" wearing fins, scales, and gills. Eighteen years of so-called education simply trained me to become a fish. Oh, how proud I was till I met Your Divine Grace, who in just thirty minutes transformed my consciousness, thus saving me from a catastrophic transmigration.

I can never repay you, Śrīla Prabhupāda, for lifting me out of the miserable ocean of repeated birth and death. Although I marvel at the wonderful services offered to you by your sincere disciples, I lament my inability to do the same. I can say I have followed the four regulative principles and chanted daily sixteen rounds since the auspicious day you accepted this lowly servant. But what more have I done?

In your Baltimore lecture you stated that if you cannot do anything else, then just chant Hare Kṛṣṇa and "Jaya Śacīnandana," and dance, and that will make your life perfect. Śrīla Prabhupāda, you are so kind that you extend Lord Caitanya's benediction even to a worthless, unproductive disciple like myself. Although I have done nothing except a little chanting and dancing, I am praying that for the remainder of my life I may begin to offer you some service.

In cooperation with the Baltimore Indian community, we are planning to expand your temple into a two-story building, complete with cultural hall, stage, kitchen, and a beautiful two-thousand-square-foot temple room. We want to offer this new temple to you on July 7, 1986, the tenth anniversary of your "accidental visit" to Baltimore. Also, Śrī Śrī Nitāi-Gaurasundara will enjoy the combined celebration of Their tenth anniversary in Baltimore, and Their five-hundredth appearance day, by moving into an opulent new residence.

Externally this may appear like an increase on our part, but actually our activity remains the same: simply chanting Nitāi-Gaura and dancing. We can't really do anything else. This may be all we can ever do for you. Śrīla Prabhupāda, you are the real motivating force behind all our preaching activities, i.e., book distribution, temple construction, devotee making, *prasādam* distribution, etc.

We will try hard to serve you and increase more and more, until our nothing becomes a little something.

(Written by Mahānidhi Swami)

*Your fallen servants,*

**Disciples of Śrīla Prabhupāda**

Mahānidhi Swami / Akhiladhāra dāsa

**Disciples of Śrīla Prabhupāda and present ISKC ON** ***ācāryas***

Uddhava dāsa

**Granddisciples**

Rājarṣi dāsa / Bāla Kṛṣṇa dāsa / Sampradāya dāsa / Dharmarāja dāsa / Lakṣmī-Nārāyaṇa dāsa / Param Dhāma dāsa / Guru dāsa / Kāliya Kṛṣṇa dāsa / Rāghava-prema dāsa / Bhakta Toṭa Gopīnātha / Bhakta Nick / Bhakta Lowery / Tryadhīśa-devī dāsī

## Bangkok

*Dear Śrīla Prabhupāda,*  
Please accept our humble obeisances in the dust of your lotus feet.

*nama oṁ viṣṇu-pādāya kṛṣṇa-preṣṭhāya bhū-tale*  
*śrīmate bhaktivedānta-svāmin iti nāmine*

*namas te sārasvate deve gaura-vāṇī-pracāriṇe*  
*nirviśeṣa-śūnyavādī-pāścātya-deśa-tāriṇe*

Our lives have become meaningful because of connection with you. We have been linked up with Kṛṣṇa only through you.

Now our ISKCON *ācāryas* are worshiped georgeously, given the best anywhere—only because of their intimate connection with you.

Everywhere, people are eager to welcome us, because we are coming from you. In India, people line up to buy our books, because they are written by you. In our *kīrtana,* our preaching, our Deity worship, *prasādam—*everything—we see only you.

That name, fame, and glory which is so vastly due to you, but which you carefully avoided during your manifest presence, is now coming, as the people of the world wake up to your greatness.

As millions come to bow down before you in Vṛndāvana, Māyāpur, and New Vrindaban, they will look to us for guidance on the spiritual path which you first showed.

They will respect us, expecting the highest standard of spiritual competence from us, because we are the representatives of you.

When we are prepared to receive all your blessings, you will shower your mercy on us. Now let us be ready. Being ready means: *guru-mukha-padma-vākya cittete koriyā aikya.*

"World first, zone second." Our love for you will be shown by our cooperation to please you.

Formerly, there was one leader—you. Now there are many leaders. But none can be as absolute as you. You are the standard. "Leader" means simply to carry that standard.

These zones, these temples, these devotees, this *lakṣmī—*all belongs to you, Śrīla Prabhupāda. None of it is ours. We are simply your humble servants.

When pure devotees perform *saṅkīrtana,* there is no party spirit. So our continued efforts to spread the Kṛṣṇa consciousness movement will actually be successful when we all constantly consider the common benefit of worldwide ISKCON.

All glories to your lotus feet, Śrīla Prabhupāda. Let us always keep them in our hearts.

*Your servants,*

**Disciples of Śrīla Prabhupāda**

Ilāpati dāsa / Lakṣmīśvara dāsa

**Granddisciples**

Hotra dāsa / Nṛsiṁha-tīrtha dāsa / Rūpānuga-bhakta dāsa / Sammohinī dāsī

## Benelux

*Dear Śrīla Prabhupāda.*

**We offer our respectful obeisances to His Divine Grace A. C. Bhaktivedanta Swami Prabhupāda, who, by the force of his devotional service, purified the whole world of the contamination of impersonalism and voidism.**

On the order of your spiritual master you came to the Western world, leaving Vṛndāvana, where you were completely satisfied. Who would leave the abode of the Lord, Vṛndāvana, and exchange it for a place which can be compared to hell? Why would a pure devotee of Kṛṣṇa go where everyone is simply interested in running after the sense objects, under the guidance of leaders who claim that there is no God and that religion is for fools? Only a pure devotee who possesses this genuine compassion for the Lord's parts and parcels can perform such incomprehensible activities.

Your strong faith in the words of your spiritual master and the pure desire to execute his instructions were the ingredients for success. Your presentation of the philosophy of Kṛṣṇa consciousness astounded young men and women, philosophers, scientists, and religionists alike. Before your arrival there was no clear conception of God and the process of God realization. Because of this, even the greatest rascal could claim to be God and be worshiped by similar rascals. But your descriptions of God, His pastimes. His name. His abode. His form, and His devotees made all other information seem vague or even speculative. You have given us back the memory of our eternal father, and for this we are eternally indebted to you. The world can only be united when there is the acceptance of one authority. Who can carry that burden of being the one who is giving the direction for everyone to follow? There are only two: the person who is intoxicated by false pride and illusion, making false promises and guiding everyone on the path of sense enjoyment approaching total destruction, and the Lord's pure devotee, who has no other motive than establishing God as the supreme controller, enjoyer, and friend of all living entities and the laws of God as the governing rules of the universe, which when followed bring about prosperity and total satisfaction for all living entities.

During your stay amongst us you worked very hard to lay the foundation for a new society that had to replace the society of the cheaters and the cheated. Your presence in this world has certainly changed its course, and history will tell that you started a spiritual revolution that overthrew the "false establishment." Who could imagine that this elderly *sādhu* would be the cause of the annihilation of the philosophy of materialism which governs the modern society? You came alone, a little money, a case of books, the instruction of your spiritual master, and an immense desire to distribute Kṛṣṇa consciousness.

Thanks to you, we are now chanting Hare Kṛṣṇa, worshiping the Deity, and eating *kṛṣṇa-prasādam.*

Thanks to you, we can discriminate between reality and illusion, between happiness and suffering.

Thanks to you, we have become purified and are approaching a bright future.

Śrīla Prabhupāda, thank you so much for having been merciful and given us Kṛṣṇa consciousness.

We beg to remain your disciples and granddisciples.

(Written by Jaya Gopāla dāsa)

*Your insignificant servants,*

## Benin City

All glories to His Divine Grace Śrīla Prabhupāda,  
Who journeyed to the West to bring bhāgavata-dharma!  
Stomping, dancing, and crying to the holy name,  
He personifies the leaping lion of Śrī Gaurasundara.

All glories to His Divine Grace Śrīla Prabhupāda,  
At whose feet sit all the great Vaiṣṇavas!  
By chastising the faceless, crazy logic of Kali-yuga  
He embodies the fighting strength of the Pāṇḍavas.

All glories to His Divine Grace Śrīla Prabhupāda,  
Whose Bhaktivedanta purports destroy the terrible Māyāvādī monster!  
By describing Śrī Kṛṣṇa from the Saṁhitās and Purāṇas,  
He establishes the all-blissful Supreme Personality of Godhead, Ajita.

All glories to His Divine Grace Śrīla Prabhupāda,  
Whose real joy is the bhajana of Śrī Śrī Rādhā-Madhava!  
Playing instruments such as harmonium, mṛdaṅga, and karatālas,  
He infuses attraction for the holy names Govinda, Kṛṣṇa.

All glories to His Divine Grace Śrīla Prabhupāda,  
Who has entered into the eternal pastimes of Lord Dāmodara!  
By infusing love of Hari within the hearts of his disciples,  
He personifies the perfection of Vaiṣṇava-paramparā.

*Your servants at ISKCON Benin City, Nigeria,*

Ānandinī dāsa / Mahānanda dāsa / Bali dāsa / Sādhana-bhakti dāsa / Bhakta Victor / Bhakta Clement / Bhakta Census / Bhakta Okosun / Bhakta Segun / Bhakta Taiye / Bhakta Shola

## Bhaktivedanta Ashram

Today is the appearance day of a great personality, a hero, saint, and well-wisher, Śrīla Prabhupāda.

Your words are like the rain of nectar for the fallen souls burning in the forest fire of material existence.

When a person saves others from danger, he is called a hero. You are truly a hero for picking up piles of ashes from the blazing fire, making them your disciples.

A saint is a person who acts and teaches godly behavior and the proper standard of religious life, despite opposing elements.

In a world of legal murder and relative laws, you are giving the pure truth.

You saved us, bewildered in the relative world.

A well-wisher is a person who acts for the benefit of others.

Knowing the living entity to be a spirit soul, always distributing millions of books and plates of *prasādam,* you are the true auspicious personality.

You give us *kṛṣṇa-prema,* a gift that surpasses the gold of this world.

On this auspicious day we offer our humble obeisances at your lotus feet.

(Written by Kālākṣī-devī dāsī)

## Bhaktivedanta Manor

*nama oṁ viṣṇu-pādāya kṛṣṇa-preṣṭhāya bhū-tale*  
*śrīmate bhaktivedānta-svāmin iti nāmine*

*namas te sārasvate deve gaura-vāṇī-pracāriṇe*  
*nirviśeṣa-śūnyavādī-pāścātya-deśa-tāriṇe*

*Dear Śrīla Prabhupāda,*

We offer our respectful obeisances unto you, who as *jagad-guru* appeared to perfectly fulfill the mission of the Supreme Personality of Godhead.

We offer our respectful obeisances to Śrīla Prabhupāda,  
who appeared with the mission of the four Kumāras  
and who underwent severe austerities to teach the world  
the path of devotional service to Rādhā and Kṛṣṇa.

We offer our respectful obeisances to Śrīla Prabhupāda,  
who appeared with the mission of Lord Varāha  
and who raised planet earth from its degradation,  
where it was hidden in filth by asuric exploitation.

We offer our respectful obeisances to Śrīla Prabhupāda,  
who appeared with the mission of Devarṣi Nārada  
and who traveled at will all over the globe,  
expounding the glories of the Lord's holy name.

We offer our respectful obeisances to Śrīla Prabhupāda,  
who appeared with the mission of Nara-Nārāyaṇa Ṛṣis  
and who performed exemplary penances to show  
ideal sense control achieved through bhakti-yoga.

We offer our respectful obeisances to Śrīla Prabhupāda,  
who appeared with the mission of Lord Kapila  
and who has lucidly explained material existence  
and freedom from distress in transcendental life.

We offer our respectful obeisances to Śrīla Prabhupāda,  
who appeared with the mission of Lord Dattātreya  
and who spoke on the subject of transcendence,  
especially instructing his intimate disciples.

We offer our respectful obeisances to Śrīla Prabhupāda,  
who appeared with the mission of Lord Yajña  
and who, in the absence of trained leaders,  
managed ISKCON, assisted by his sons and daughters.

We offer our respectful obeisances to Śrīla Prabhupāda,  
who appeared with the mission of Mahārāja Ṛṣabhadeva  
and who is honored by all orders of life  
as the crest jewel amongst paramahaṁsas.

We offer our respectful obeisances to Śrīla' Prabhupāda,  
who appeared with the mission of Mahārāja Pṛthu  
and who revealed the perfection of leadership,  
social organization, and prosperity from the land.

We offer our respectful obeisances to Śrīla Prabhupāda,  
who appeared with the mission of Lord Matsya  
and who rescued the Vedas from being submerged  
in the speculative interpretations of so-called scholars.

We offer our respectful obeisances to Śrīla Prabhupāda,  
who appeared with the mission of Lord Kūrma  
and who is the support of the endeavors of his disciples  
to churn the ocean of love of Godhead.

We offer our respectful obeisances to Śrīla Prabhupāda,  
who appeared with the mission of Lord Dhanvantari  
and whose fame is spread all over the three worlds  
for bringing the cure for repeated birth and death.

We offer our respectful obeisances to Śrīla Prabhupāda,  
who appeared with the mission of Śrī Mohinī-mūrti  
and who allured the demons by his charm  
and used their science and technology to defeat them.

We offer our respectful obeisances to Śrīla Prabhupāda,  
who appeared with the mission of Lord Nṛsiṁha-deva  
and whose remembrance by his devotees frees them from all fear  
in the face of atrocities threatened by godless materialists.

We offer our respectful obeisances to Śrīla Prabhupāda,  
who appeared with the mission of Lord Vāmanadeva  
and who traversed the world to regain it from the demons,  
who ultimately will gratefully surrender at his lotus feet.

We offer our respectful obeisances to Śrīla Prabhupāda,  
who appeared with the mission of Lord Paraśurāma  
and who offers opportunity for the government of nations  
to take advice from first-class brāhmaṇas or face inevitable ruination.

We offer our respectful obeisances to Śrīla Prabhupāda,  
who appeared with the mission of Śrīla Vyāsadeva  
and who, seeing that the people of the twentieth century lacked intelligence,  
presented beautiful editions of Vedic literature, understandable by scholars and common men.

We offer our respectful obeisances to Śrīla Prabhupāda,  
who appeared with the mission of Lord Rāmacandra  
and who crossed the ocean to reclaim Lakṣmīdevī  
and is the example of ideal leader, father, friend, and spiritual master.  
We offer our respectful obeisances to Śrīla Prabhupāda,  
who appeared with the mission of Lord Balarāma  
and who, at the request of the paramparā, descended to relieve mother Bhūmi  
from the burden of a misdirected civilization addicted to abominable activities.  
We offer our respectful obeisances to Śrīla Prabhupāda,  
who appeared with the mission of Lord Kṛṣṇa  
and who performed many extraordinary pastimes, defeating the demons,  
protecting the devotees, and establishing the principles of real religion.  
We offer our respectful obeisances to Śrīla Prabhupāda,  
who appeared with the mission of Lord Buddha  
and who is continuing the progressive revelation of the ācāryas  
and is the savior for those who were meat-eaters and faithless to scriptural regulations.  
We offer our respectful obeisances to Śrīla Prabhupāda,  
who appeared with the mission of Lord Kalki  
and who, riding upon his movement, cuts down all demoniac mentality,  
warding off Kali, to usher in a new age of Satya-yuga.  
We offer our respectful obeisances to Śrīla Prabhupāda,  
who appeared with the mission of Lord Caitanya  
and who worships the Lord by the performance of saṅkīrtana-yajña,  
accompanied by his associates, disciples, granddisciples, life members, and all friends of ISKCON.

Śrīla Prabhupāda *ki jaya!*

(Written by Akhaṇḍadhī dāsa)

*We beg to remain your humble servants, the devotees at Bhaktivedanta Manor,*

## Bhaktivedanta Village

*nama oṁ viṣṇu-pādāya kṛṣṇa-preṣṭhāya bhū-tale*  
*śrīmate bhaktivedānta-svāmin iti nāmine*

*namas te sārasvate deve gaura-vāṇī-pracāriṇe*  
*nirviśeṣa-śūnyavādī-pāścātya-deśa-tāriṇe*

*Srīla Prabhupāda dayā karaha vicāra*  
*vicāra karile citte pābe camatkāra*

**Anyone interested in logic and argument should apply them to the mercy of His Divine Grace A. C. Bhaktivedanta Swami Prabhupāda. If he does so he will find it strikingly wonderful.**

*Dear Śrīla Prabhupāda.*

Every day we worship you by praying, *śrī-guru-caraṇa-padma, kevala-bhakati-sadma:* "The lotus feet of the spiritual master are the abode of pure devotional service." Śrīla Rūpa Gosvāmī analyzes pure devotional service as having six essential characteristics: it brings immediate relief from all material distress, it is the beginning of all auspiciousness, it automatically puts one in transcendental happiness, it is rarely achieved, it is transcendental even to liberation, and it is the only means to attract Kṛṣṇa. This supreme fortune is available only through the lotus feet of Your Divine Grace.

Śrīla Viśvanātha Cakravartī Ṭhākura teaches, *yasya prasādād bhagavat-prasādaḥ:* "The mercy of the spiritual master makes one eligible to receive Kṛṣṇa's mercy." Only the spiritual master can give any opportunity to serve Kṛṣṇa. *Śrīmad-Bhāgavatam* instructs that one must learn the science of devotional service from a pure spiritual master after accepting him as one's life and soul and worshipable deity. In fact, the *Bhāgavatam* repeatedly asserts that devotional service is not available to anyone who has not taken on his head the dust of the lotus feet of a *mahājana* like Your Divine Grace. In Lord Caitanya's teachings to Sanātana Gosvāmī on the scientific process of devotional service to Kṛṣṇa, the five preliminary items all refer to the spiritual master.

I. *guru-pādāśraya—*one must accept the shelter of a bona fide spiritual master.

2\. *dīkṣā—*one must take initiation from him.

3\. *guru-sevana—*one must serve the spiritual master according to his (the *guru's)* desire.

4\. *sad-dharma-śikṣā—*one must receive instructions from the spiritual master and inquire from him in order to learn devotional service.

5\. *sādhu-mārgānugamana—*one must follow in the footsteps of the previous *ācāryas* and strictly follow the directions given by the *guru.*

Any service offered to Kṛṣṇa must be offered through a spiritual master whose heart is completely pure, free from all material affection. Only then is our attempt at service acceptable to Kṛṣṇa. Kṛṣṇa says in the *Bhagavad-gītā* that He accepts any simple offering, but only if offered with love and devotion by one whose consciousness is pure. Your consciousness is pure Kṛṣṇa consciousness—*kṛṣṇa-bhakti-rasa-bhāvita.* We fallen souls can approach the supreme pure only through Your Divine Grace.

Śrīla Prabhupāda, you are the supreme professor of the science of devotional service. Anyone who claims to know anything about Kṛṣṇa must have heard it from you. The presence of any piety in the world today is due only to your accepting the order of your spiritual master to preach Śrī Caitanya Mahāprabhu's message throughout the world as your life and soul. We have all inherited this same task from you. You have left your mission unfinished only so we can spend our lives trying to complete it. We are your limbs in the world. Apart from you, our lives have no meaning. Apart from your service, we have no life. The occasion of Vyāsa-pūjā is an opportunity for us to renew our commitment to your service. As we approach the five-hundredth anniversary of Śrī Caitanya Mahāprabhu's appearance, we, your helpless children and grandchildren, aspire to dedicate our work, words, and thoughts to establishing Lord Caitanya and you. His greatest servant, as the worshipable deities for all mankind for the next nine thousand five hundred years. We pray-that you will help us remain strong in that commitment and that we may never stray from the shelter of your lotus feet.

*Your servants at Bhaktivedanta Village,*

**Disciples of Śrīla Prabhupāda**

## Bologna

*Dear Śrīla Prabhupāda.*

Please accept my prostrated humble obeisances. Thank you very much for having saved me. I can never repay this eternal debt. As you say, "Simply preach vigorously." This is the gratitude we can offer for your blessings. The opportunity to meet a pure devotee of the Lord is very rare, yet you gave yourself freely, without hindrances, just to bless the poor conditioned souls. Unfortunately, I have to struggle just to find words to glorify you. Even though your countless qualities cannot be glorified even by thousands of mouths, still I find great purification simply by this attempt.

The quickest way to receive your mercy is to take up the risk in spreading Kṛṣṇa consciousness. I can see that all the intimate disciples of yours who have taken up this risk have become greatly advanced by your kind blessings.

You possess in full the qualities of a pure devotee of the Lord. Your sharp voice kills all the impurities within the heart. The only solace for my burning heart is to hear your sharp voice cutting all misconceptions into atomic parts. Unlimitedly I must thank you, Śrīla Prabhupāda. You are the destroyer of all illusion and encourage everyone to take up devotional service. The essence of *Śrīmad-Bhāgavatam* is to develop love of God. How can this be done? By listening to your instructions. The most difficult achievement will become easy with your perfect guidance. I feel completely protected by your voice. Now it is the time to manifest this gratitude by dovetailing all my senses in your service. This will please Kṛṣṇa very much. As you said, "I am happy when I see that you are struggling for Kṛṣṇa."

Pleasing you means pleasing Kṛṣṇa. Pleasing Kṛṣṇa means pleasing you. My life's goal is simply always to think of you, how I can please you in a better way, always improving. I pray to you again and again, Śrīla Prabhupāda, to allow me to serve you by organizing book distribution more and more to purify my heart.

A long time has passed without any fixed engagement. Now on this blissful and most blessed event I pray that with full determination my mind will become steady in your service, praying this at the dust of your pure, divine lotus feet, to become your instrument in spreading Kṛṣṇa consciousness.

*Your servants,*

Durgama dāsa / Nāma-prabhu dāsa / Rāsarāja dāsa / Bhakta Albino / Bhakta Giuseppe / Bhakta Darko / Bhakta Silvano / Subhadrā-devī dāsī / Mahānāma-devī dāsī / Mahāśṛṅga-devī dāsī / Bhaktin Raffaella

## Bombay

*Dear Śrīla Prabhupāda,*

Please accept our most fallen obeisances at your lotus-like feet.

On this auspicious day, we reconfirm our desire to serve your mission to give the prime benediction of Kṛṣṇa's devotional service to all living entities. Surrender to your instructions is the secret to success. When will this surrender manifest itself, so the mind, body, and intelligence will be actively spreading your ISKCON movement intended to deliver the whole world?

For this purpose, Śrīla Prabhupāda, you have given us so much facility—temples, books, pastimes, prasādam, and endless other opportunities to be Kṛṣṇa conscious and to spread Kṛṣṇa consciousness. We certainly cannot say you have not given us the things necessary for this. Please continue to shower your mercy so that we can maintain everything nicely.

## Boston

*Dear Śrīla Prabhupāda,*

Please accept our most humble obeisances at your lotus feet, which are the shelter for all conditioned souls and are the nectarean source of bliss for all of the devotees of Lord Kṛṣṇa.

O Śrīla Prabhupāda, voidism and impersonalism are permeating every aspect of this Kali-yuga society, creating havoc and suffering, but by association with you, who are the embodiment of pure devotional service to the Supreme Lord, Śrī Kṛṣṇa, the whole world is becoming imbued with knowledge of the Absolute Truth and can thus be freed from the miseries of material existence. We therefore offer thousands of obeisances unto your lotus feet.

Our obeisances are unto you, who can, by your causeless mercy, turn the iron-framed hearts of *mlecchas* and *yavanas* into gold. Those golden-hearted devotees will in turn deliver your mercy to others.

Again and again our obeisances are unto you, O *jagad-guru,* who for the first time in Vaiṣṇava history have distributed Kṛṣṇa consciousness in all parts of the world, to all classes of men, thus making the holy name of Kṛṣṇa heard in every town and village.

We offer our obeisances unto you, Śrīla Prabhupāda. Your transcendental pastimes are the source of inspiration for all devotees. When we hear those pastimes, love for you wells up in our hearts, and our determination to please you becomes renewed and ever-increasing. Those pastimes are sweeter than the sweetest mango.

Śrīla Prabhupāda, on this occasion of your auspicious appearance day, we beg for but one benediction. Please reveal to us, your disciples and granddisciples, the proper way to execute your will, free from quarrel and dissension, so that we may fulfill your vision for pushing on this great ISKCON movement for the next ten thousand years.

(Written by Puruṣārtha dāsa)

*Your eternal servants in New England,*

## Boulder

*Dear Śrīla Prabhupāda,*

Please accept our humble obeisances at the dust of your lotus feet.

By your transcendental order and mercy, we now find ourselves preaching in a college town, in name a center for higher learning, in reality a bastion for those of deluded intelligence.

Here, brandishing the flags of economic development, philosophical speculation, high-tech material manipulation, and sensual delights, hundreds of self-proclaimed "men of knowledge" propagate a system of life destined to be ultimately meaningless and therefore futile. By your mercy, we are now able to see all this clearly.

Marching with well-practiced self-composure, tens of thousands of youths follow their foolish teachers with unquestioning confidence, knowing full well (although falsely) that success, happiness, fame, and fortune await them in exchange for their submissive service. This, too, you have allowed us to see, with the penetrating vision of the *śāstra.*

Here, the cumulative energy of a whole nation, nay, a whole generation, is mixed, molded, and perfectly adjusted to fulfill the supreme needs of the belly, the genitals, and the mind. Looking ahead with vision fixed on a lifetime of opportunity, they have actually been prepared only for slaughter. Horrible slaughter. This, too, you have shown us.

And it is not easy for them! They are all working, struggling, and worrying so much! Always full of anxiety and fear, always lamenting for illusory trivia, always trying to bolster their belittled sense of self, while suffering defeat at every step. And yet they go on! On and on and on. Day after day, year after year, lifetime after lifetime.

As we once did. As we were destined to do. And as we should still be doing, if you had not appeared.

And, as it once seemed to us, now it seems to them. Too simple—far too simple. How is it possible that just by chanting, just by eating *prasādam,* and just by loving Kṛṣṇa all the anomalies of the world can be adjusted? Not possible!

Yet, as you told us, we now tell them. "It is a fact." And it is! And of this we are all living proof.

So daily we go out chanting, distributing your books, distributing *prasādam,* teaching them how to cook for Kṛṣṇa, teaching them how to think for Kṛṣṇa, teaching them how to live for Kṛṣṇa, teaching them how just to love Kṛṣṇa. Because we know—because you have shown us—this is the remedy, the cure-all and the end-all. This you have shown us, and we can never thank you or repay you enough.

Please empower us to show them just as you have shown us. So simple, yet so sublime. Please, Śrīla Prabhupāda, please empower us to show them, just as you have shown us.

(Written by Anuttama dāsa)

*Your lowly servants of Boulder Yātrā,*

**Disciples of Śrīla Prabhupāda**

## Brisbane

*nama oṁ viṣṇu-pādāya kṛṣṇa-preṣṭhāya bhū-tale*  
*śrīmate bhaktivedānta-svāmin iti nāmine*

*namas te sārasvate deve gaura-vāṇī-pracāriṇe*  
*nirviśeṣa-śūnyavādī-pāścātya-deśa-tāriṇe*

*Dear Śrīla Prabhupāda,*

TODAY, from this far and distant place, I'm writing you a letter. You are our master and friend, and so, in your physical absence, we are feeling very vacant and lonely. It is wonderful, however, that as I write I'm able to remember you again—how you appeared before us, dancing and chanting, feasting and preaching, enthusing us to purify our hearts in devotional service. And as I remember, the transcendental separation is eased.

Even when you walked among us, we were all mostly somewhere else doing service, and just to have your personal association seemed as dear to us as life. Fortunately, those times have been kept very close within our hearts, and those pastimes of mercy will never fade from memory.

You personified the Vedic conclusions, and for those who knew you, even for a moment, that association created an eternal bond to the lotus feet of the Lord. You transformed our vision by forcing, teaching, and allowing us to serve you, just as touchstone turns darkness to light, lust to love, matter to spirit.

I have thus come to know there is no other way except the path of *śrī-guru-sevā*, for this is the pure and full disciplic mercy of Kṛṣṇa. You forced, taught, and then allowed me to serve you, and that service, insignificant as it was, I remember so clearly. The intrinsic nature of that service will never die. It will continue forever, even beyond the death of this worthless body.

So as I write, I am finding myself lost—lost in memories of you. Thank you for these memories, and thank you for living among us. One fine day, when they read your books, the whole world will stop and thank you.

Śrīla Prabhupāda *ki jaya!*

(Written by Veṇugopāla dāsa)

*Your humble servants-of Brisbane Yātrā, Australia*

## Cairns

*Dear Śrīla Prabhupāda,*

Please accept our humble obeisances unto your divine lotus feet. All glories to Your Divine Grace, for you have come and delivered us from the darkness of the darkest ignorance. Your magnanimity knows no bounds, and the good fortune of all of us who have received your precious gift is unparalleled. We pray that we may always be fixed in assisting you in your divine mission, which is bringing about a revolution in the consciousness of this world's misdirected civilization.

You have taught us that we should always set our mentality on winning Kṛṣṇa. You have taught us how we must force ourselves to conform to this strict standard of pure devotional service. Actually, Śrīla Prabhupāda, you have taught us everything. How can we repay you? You have saved us from the most hellish condition of material existence. Because we have Such little realization, such little faith and love and compassion, we wonder how we will be able to follow your perfect example of selfless dedication to the saṅkīrtana mission of Lord Caitanya. We can only fall down in the dust of your lotus feet and beg you to please allow us to preach your message, and always remain the humble servants of your servants.

*Jaya* Śrīla Prabhupāda!

**Disciples of Śrīla Prabhupāda and present ISKCON** ***ācāryas***

Vrajarāja dāsa

## Calcutta

An attempted appreciation offered as homage  
unto the lotus feet of our Śrīla Prabhupāda  
We walk the many thoroughfares of life  
Aimless and way lost—  
Drifting on time's tide  
On waves of circumstance.  
In some arms we have flown in skies  
On others crawled beneath the earth,  
Timeless, seems our origins—  
Lost in the surge of the ages.  
Propelled along the cosmos  
In one form or another—  
Birth after birth  
Pursued by death—  
Relentless, unforgiving.  
Enslaved by our senses,  
Animal impulses,  
Conditioned by forgetfulness.  
Until. . . perchance, when we attain a human birth  
We stop, think, " Whence came I?  
Wherefore am I headed?"  
Then, perhaps, we seek  
A surer path, and question  
The answers of the sages.  
Yet many are misdirected  
By wayward speculation  
Empirics in experimentation,  
Others in impersonal self-annihilation.  
It is hard then to understand why  
We, unseeking, unseeing, undeserving,  
Were favored by one such as yourself,  
Śrīla Prabhupāda.  
Your oceanic compassion,  
Your magnanimity unbounding,  
Could bear not the sight  
Of our intolerable strife.  
And so in our midst you descended  
From Grace's realm sublime.

We were bound by our nomadic minds' whims,  
Trapped in karma's inescapable web.  
You delivered us from our throes  
On Māyā's stagnant shores  
And told us we could be masters of destiny.  
Bewildered by attraction, aversion, pleasure and pain,  
Insane in lust and anger and greed,  
we were traversing the path  
To the gates of hell.  
You equipped us with a quiver  
Full of arrows of pointed intellect  
Guided by the bow of paramparā wisdom.  
You said, as you traveled ahead,  
Carrying the load of our sins,  
"Fly out now in the chariot  
Of this Kṛṣṇa consciousness movement,  
Transcend the maze  
Of this dense forest of delusion. "  
And we were guided by the flicker less flame  
Of your own faith's fidelity—  
The greatest shining example.

Yet along the path,  
Ambushed by doubt,  
Overpowered by weakness,  
Unbalanced by attraction, again  
We fall into the void of forgetfulness.  
So we pray—this day  
And always—  
"Śrīla Prabhupāda, our dearest master,  
May we fear only forgetfulness of the strife  
Of the world we try to leave behind—  
This dark, dark condemned realm  
Of forgetfulness  
Of truth and Kṛṣṇa."  
Please fill us, ever,  
With the burning desire to hold  
This chance of a lifetime  
After many lifetimes of chance,  
Always aware our efforts are useless  
Without your grace and clemency.

Truly, Śrīla Prabhupāda,  
Beloved savior,  
Ineffable is the magnitude of your compassion.  
Ineffaceable is our debt to you.

*Praying to be in constant remembrance,*

*Your ever-grateful and love-bound disciples and granddisciples,*

## Canberra

*Dear Śrīla Prabhupāda.*

Please accept my humble obeisances in the dust of your lotus feet.

When I joined the movement in January 1976, your presence was very strongly felt, and you were very much alive and living with the devotees in our humble Buckingham St. (Sydney) temple. Conditions were harsh, but we could understand that material life was full of various miseries, and by performing devotional service relief could be gained from conditional existence. We continued on.

In those days, your movement was pretty well established on a worldwide basis, so close association with you was hard to achieve. On being transferred to Melbourne, a colder and more austere place, I was fortunate to have that closer association in June of 1976. The pure devotee was coming; everyone was jubilant just by thinking of your visit. The minds and hearts of everyone were softening. Devotees would be singing, and some laughing, for no apparent reason. This is the potency of the pure servitor of the Lord. Even the holy places become purified by your presence.

I remember watching your every gesture during classes. So much authority and expertise! Your greeting the Deities, walking across the courtyard, going into "Prabhupāda House."

We had a special *darśana* and were told to bring a gift. I had no money, so I begged from door to door some flowers and money. When I gave them to you I was touched by your hand, feeling myself very fortunate. I felt very fortunate that such a rare event should happen to me. After you left, things slowly went back to normal—the routine, the regulation.

I didn't see you again until in India in January 1977, when by the mercy of the devotees I was able to leave Australia before the festival started and travel around with you for a few weeks. We met you in Calcutta after you arrived there from the Kumba-melā. I was able to assist in cleaning your rooms. We traveled to Bhuvaneśvara, where you were to lay the cornerstone for the new temple. I was able to see you more closely, relaxed, living simply in a mud hut, everyone using one tap. I cleaned your rooms, kept your car with the *tilaka* mark on the bonnet clean and polished, went on morning walks, listened to your *darśanas.* By your kindness I received my first initiation, after which one *sannyāsī* asked me, "Who are you?" to which I replied *"braham anasmi."* not even knowing basic things, yet you still initiated me.

The next day I went out to get some *dakṣiṇā* for you by begging in the street in Purī. I liberated 46 rupees. When I came back that evening I managed to get in to see you alone, and I gave you the 46 rupees plus $5.00 I had been saving for a rainy day. You were so glorious and effulgent sitting there, and yet you humbly accepted the measly offering with gratitude. You said, "Thank you very much. Where did you get so much?"

Other things happened, but this was the last time I was to see you so closely.

I want to thank you, Śrīla Prabhupāda, not only for the association but for everything you have given—the devotees, the books, the temples, the festivals, the *prasādam,* and especially Kṛṣṇa Himself. My realization has not increased over the years, but I have been fortunate to have gained a greater appreciation for what you have given and the effect you have had and will have for thousands of years to come.

(Written by Pauruṣa dāsa)

*We beg to remain your servants,*

**Disciples of Śrīla Prabhupāda**

## Caracas

*Dear Śrīla Prabhupāda,*

On this most joyful and blessed day of your transcendental appearance, I can just remember your transcendental words and activities. This is a blessing from you so that I may become purified from material contamination and in this way become attached to the sacred dust of your lotus feet, which are a secure shelter for fallen souls like myself.

When you visited the Caracas temple and gave us your causeless mercy by showing us pure devotional service, you were sitting on your *vyāsāsana* and gazing with love at Their Lordships Śrī Śrī Gaura-nitāi, and you began to chant *"parama-karuṇa pahun dvi-jana,"* and tears came out of your eyes .... Everyone was moved at the genuine manifestation of transcendental ecstasy, and our stonelike hearts melted by seeing your pure love for Lord Caitanya and Nityānanda. You told us, "You are very fortunate to have these two Prabhus here; now make others fortunate too."

Once, on a morning walk in the park, we were walking and chanting on our beads. Suddenly you wanted to sit down, and we sat at your lotus feet. Everybody was silent, waiting for you to speak, but you just looked at us for a while and then said, "Just chant Hare Kṛṣṇa!" and started to walk again.

A lawyer who was invited to come began to ask you about the scarcity of food in the world, and you showed him a bird eating fruits from a tree and explained to him how Kṛṣṇa is maintaining all living entities and how the leaders of society mismanage the property of Kṛṣṇa due to lack of God consciousness. The lawyer was very impressed and appreciative. Then you started to explain how modern civilization wastes so much time in creating artificial needs when they could instead be developing spiritual life . . . There are so many instances that I can't remember now that make me feel intense separation from you.

I remember the example you gave once that if you want to be always in the sunshine you always have to follow the sun, and in this way one will not experience darkness. So, in the same way, by always following your brilliant instructions, which are like *cintāmaṇi* stones, we will always keep ourselves in the blissful light of your loving and divine presence.

Please keep me at your lotus feet. Correct me when, following my mind, I deviate from your instructions.

This ISKCON movement is an extension of your body, and your disciples and granddisciples are your expansions as well. Only by your mercy can I understand this and engage myself in the preaching of your glories and teachings, which alone can deliver the whole world from repeated birth and death.

Śrīla Prabhupāda, you are my eternal father. Please forgive this foolish son of yours and accept me again and again. Otherwise I have no shelter.

(Written by Jagat-cakṣus dāsa)

Your servants,

**Disciples of Śrīla Prabhupāda**

## Chandigarh

Māyā's mud—what a drudge  
Peaches and cream, ice cream and fudge  
It's all poison, Śrīla Prabhupāda, unless we offer it to you.  
You are the one who has changed the old into the new.  
Chapana Bhoga\* without Tulasī the Lord does not accept.  
For us, you are that Tulasī leaf, Śrīla Prabhupāda. Let us all not forget.  
It's all poison, Śrīla Prabhupāda, unless we offer it to you.  
You are the one who has changed the old into the new.  
By Rāma's name alone Hanumān's stones did / loot.  
By your name alone we succeed, Śrīla Prabhupāda. Shameful if we gloat.  
It's all poison, Śrīla Prabhupāda, unless we offer it to you.  
You are the one who has changed the old into the new.

Kṛṣṇa to Brahmā, Nārada and Vyāsa, you come calling.  
You are the missing link, Śrīla Prabhupāda, changing animals to human beings.  
It's all poison, Śrīla Prabhupāda, unless we offer it to you.  
You are the one who has changed the old into the new.

Caurasi\*\* to sannyasi, Sanskriti and guru, too.  
A miracle. Without your mercy, Śrīla Prabhupāda, what could we do?  
It's all poison, Śrīla Prabhupāda, unless we offer it to you.  
You are the one who has changed the old into the new.

You taught us how to live, to give, and to forgive.  
Your order "Now make others fortunate, "eternal instructions to live.  
It's all poison, Śrīla Prabhupāda, unless we offer it to you.  
You are the one who has changed the old into the new.

As Lakṣmaṇa told Sītā to stay in his mystic circle  
You protect us by ISKCON. Whoever leaves, his life is danger full.  
It's all poison, Śrīla Prabhupāda, unless we offer it to you.  
You are the one who has changed the old into the new.

Nitya-mukta, hero of the material and spiritual skies,  
We are all nitya-baddha, Śrīla Prabhupāda. Please cut our material ties.  
It's all poison, Śrīla Prabhupāda, unless we offer it to you.  
You are the one who has changed the old into the new.

Disciples, Granddisciples, and forever after that  
You will be our password, Śrīla Prabhupāda, and that's a fact.  
It's all poison, Śrīla Prabhupāda, unless we offer it to you.  
You are the one who has changed the old into the new.

## Chicago

*Dear Śrīla Prabhupāda,*

Please accept our most humble obeisances at your lotus feet. On this day we are simply trying to appreciate your appearance in this world and the great fortune you have brought upon our lives. The scriptures state that only a very fortunate living entity, who by force of *karma* is wandering throughout the universe, gets the opportunity to associate with a bona fide spiritual master by the grace of Kṛṣṇa. Then, by the combined mercy *of guru* and Kṛṣṇa, he gets the seed of the creeper of devotion. We were unfortunate and undeserving, but you simply attracted us, Śrīla Prabhupāda.

As the Supreme Lord appears in this world and performs superhuman pastimes, so also does His pure devotee, who has come just to execute His mission. The *guru* comes to deliver the unchanging message of the Absolute Personality of Godhead for the supreme benefit of the fallen conditioned souls. He comes to teach us by the example of his pure character full of transcendental qualities. Unlike the conditioned soul, who takes birth in this world with purpose of enjoyment through his mind and senses, the *ācārya* purely engages his mind and senses for the service and pleasure of the Absolute Sentient Personality of Godhead.

As you said of your Guru Mahārāja, "He appears before us to reveal the light of the *Vedas* and to bestow upon us the blessings of full-fledged freedom, after which we should hanker at every step of our life's journey."

O, Prabhupāda, please bless us with that same intense eagerness you have for the service of the Lord and His devotees. We are your servants and will be happy only when we make your desire our desire. Please purify our smudged and crippled hearts. We are like selfish children, and you are our father. Prabhupāda, you once said that our relationship with you is established according to our "submission" to your will. Please give us strong faith in your words and the holy name of Kṛṣṇa; we have no knowledge or devotion.

*yasya deve parā bhaktir*  
*yathā deve tathā gurau*  
*tasyaite kathitā hy arthāḥ*  
*prakāśante mahātmanaḥ*

**"Only unto those great souls who simultaneously have implicit faith in both the Lord and the spiritual master are all the imports of Vedic knowledge automatically revealed."** ***(Śvetāśvatara Upaniṣad)***

If we can just *hear* the words of the *Gītā* and *Bhāgavatam* with your purports, then we will become happy in blissful Kṛṣṇa consciousness and will be able to serve you in pure devotion.

*nitya-siddha kṛṣṇa-premā sadhya kabhu naya*  
*śravaṇādi-śuddha-citte karaye udaya*  
*(Cc. Madhya 22.l07)*

**"Pure love for Kṛṣṇa is eternally established in the hearts of living entities. It is not something to be gained from another source. When the heart is purified by hearing and chanting, the living entity naturally awakens."**

Śrīla Prabhupāda, out of kindness you enable us to easily associate with you through your books, but we are so unfortunate that we have no attraction for them. Prabhupāda, please let us give up envy and false independence and surrender to you. Lord Caitanya has instructed,

*kṛṣṇa-bhakti-janma-mūla haya sādhu-saṅga*  
*kṛṣṇa-prema janme, teṅho punaḥ mukhya aṅga*

**"The root cause of devotional service to Lord Kṛṣṇa is association with advanced devotees. Even when one's dormant love for Kṛṣṇa awakens, association with devotees is still most essential."**

Śrīla Prabhupāda, you are certainly Lord Kṛṣṇa's divine grace upon us. You are an "ocean of mercy, friend of the poor, and the lord and master of the devotees." Please give us the strength to take this mercy ourselves and distribute it to all others. We who are fallen in this ocean of birth and death bow down before you on this auspicious day and beg your mercy that we be freed from our present causeless unwillingness to serve the Lord and all souls.

(Written by Pṛthuśravā dāsa)

*Your servants at Chicago ISKCON.*

## Christchurch

*nama oṁ viṣṇu-pādāya kṛṣṇa-preṣṭhāya bhū-tale*  
*śrīmate bhaktivedānta-svāmin iti nāmine*

*namas te sārasvate deve gaura-vāṇī-pracāriṇe*  
*nirviśeṣa-śūnyavādī-pāścātya-deśa-tāriṇe*

*Dear Śrīla Prabhupāda,*

Please allow us to offer some words of appreciation for the matchless gifts that you have given us.

Before Your Divine Grace set foot in America in 1965, the Western world was gripped unshakably by irreligion and impersonalism. It is now twenty years since you first set out to bless the world with Lord Caitanya's mercy. In that short amount of time millions of fortunate living beings have been blessed by contacting your International Society for Krishna Consciousness. In so many wonderful ways you have showered your causeless mercy upon the fallen souls.

Even those who cannot or will not chant or read your transcendental books are relishing the taste of *kṛṣṇa-prasādam.* In this way your numerous secret weapons are destroying the hard hearts of the stubborn materialists.

May we never forget Your Divine Grace even for a moment, nor your instructions. Please allow us, your children and grandchildren, to humbly assist you in your mission.

(Written by Alalanatha dāsa)

*Your most grateful servants in Christchurch,*

**Disciples of Śrīla Prabhupāda and present ISKCON** ***ācāryas***

## Cincinnati

*Dear Śrīla Prabhupāda,*

Please accept our humble obeisances in the dust of your holy lotus feet. We have just recently opened this center here in Cincinnati in a humble and helpless attempt to please you. We are praying *on* this auspicious day of your appearance that you will be kind upon us and smile at our childish attempts to preach. We are like small children trying to follow a great and mighty father. You traveled all over the world like a powerful, conquering hero, conquering the hearts and minds of millions of conditioned souls wherever you went. We are falling all over each other just trying to keep ourselves out of *māyā.* You translated the most wonderful books, gave the most wonderful lectures (when you talked about Kṛṣṇa, it opened our hearts), opened temples, put on festivals. You revealed so many different ways to spread Kṛṣṇa consciousness. Yet we can hardly accomplish even the most basic instructions that you gave us. But if you are not pleased with us, we are lost. Our only hope is that you will take pity on us and shower your rain of mercy on our heads. We are foolish, helpless, lost children, and without your help and kindness we will remain as such. We are inadequate in every respect. Please give us your mercy so that we can become worthy of being your servants. We pray that on your next Vyāsa-pūjā we will have a longer list of devotees and a bigger book score to offer at your lotus feet. All glories to you, Śrīla Prabhupāda, for saving us from *māyā*! Please never let us go away from your lotus feet. Jaya Prabhupāda!!!

*Your eternal servants,*

Śrutadeva dāsa / Janakanātha dāsa / Ikṣvāku dāsa / Bāla Kṛṣṇa dāsa / Kevala-bhakti dāsa / Śuddha-bhakta dāsa / Jaya Ṛṣi dāsa / Daṇḍakeśa-devī dāsī / Mādhavī-devī dāsī / Rādhāpriyā-devī dāsī

## Columbus

*Dear Śrīla Prabhupāda.*

Please accept our most humble obeisances at your divine lotus feet. You are truly the lord and master of the devotees.

Each day, as we offer rose petals to you during *guru-pūjā,* an endless list of your matchless gifts to us races through our minds, softening our hearts and wetting our eyes. You gave these gifts without expecting anything in return, asking only that we might accept them. Oh, that a genuine appreciation of those gifts may awaken within the cores of our sleeping souls! By Lord Kṛṣṇa's grace, we now beg to acknowledge just a few of those gifts.

The greatest of all the gifts is the "Great Chant for Deliverance": Hare Kṛṣṇa, Hare Kṛṣṇa, Kṛṣṇa Kṛṣṇa, Hare Hare / Hare Rāma, Hare Rāma, Rāma Rāma, Hare Hare. The Vedic scriptures repeatedly emphasize that no other method of spiritual realization can be as effective in the present age as the chanting of this *mahā-mantra.* The sincere chanting of the holy names of God revives our natural consciousness of unalloyed love for Kṛṣṇa and mitigates the hostile atmosphere of Kali-yuga, the Age of Quarrel.

You have translated for the world a unique library, comprising over seventy volumes of profound Vedic wisdom, as it is, without any personally motivated interpretations. In the volumes are practical instructions for respiritualizing the entire world through philosophy, the sciences, music, dance, painting, sculpture, drama, film, diet, and more.

You have given us the ecstatic service of distributing your divine books to every town and village. These transcendental time bombs are exploding the materialistic myths of the origin and purpose of life.

You have given us your wonderful ISKCON movement, which is inspiring love and trust amongst the saintly peoples of the world. Your desire is that everyone work together within the framework of ISKCON, and by our efforts to fulfill that desire. Lord Caitanya's mission is victorious all over the world in changing the hearts of the demons.

You have given us your perfect example of purity by never compromising the religious principles of no meat-eating, no gambling, no intoxication, and no illicit sex life. Our minds and hearts are becoming purified, and we are engaging the enemy with renewed spiritual vitality, expertise, and conviction.

You have given us hundreds of temples worldwide in which we are able to render some small service to the Deities of Rādhā-Kṛṣṇa, thereby always reminding ourselves of the person for whom we are fighting, and why.

You have given us the transcendental art of cooking for Kṛṣṇa, by which we can become freed from all the reactions of past sinful life and simultaneously develop strong bodies and sharp intelligence. *Prasādam* is indeed the secret weapon, being activated by Lord Kṛṣṇa's saliva.

You have given us transcendental festivals filled with chanting, dancing, and feasting. By your mercy alone, the Lord of the universe leads His happy troops on parade in major cities around the globe.

You have revived the most efficient means of organizing and protecting society against the influence *of may a:* the *varṇāśrama* institution. Simple living, depending on the cows and nature's gifts, and high thinking, offering the fruits of our endeavors to the Lord, is proving that a God-centered life-style is the most realistic approach toward world peace and prosperity.

These examples are just a few of the more obvious benedictions bestowed upon us by Your Divine Grace. Thus, by constantly remembering our eternal indebtedness to you, we stand poised to do battle, armed with your causeless mercy. Thank you.

*Your eternal servants in the Columbus Yātrā,*

**Disciples of Śrīla Prabhupāda**

Karūṣa dāsa

**Granddisciples**

Buddhiyoga dāsa / Ṛṣabhadeva dāsa / Prabuddha dāsa / Bhakta Guy / Bhakta Gus / Bhakta Tim / Bhakta Howard

## Dallas

*Dear Śrīla Prabhupāda.*

In searching for a suitable way to praise you and offer ourselves at your lotus feet on this most holy of holy days, we find ourselves at a great loss. How unfortunate we are! How spiritually destitute is the Kali-yuga mind, devoid of appreciation, love, understanding, and a true sense of your mission!

Only a short time ago, we were amongst the befooled members of the society of cheaters and the cheated, and by your causeless mercy you rescued us from this masquerade of society, friendship, and love and situated us in the eternal reality of Śrī Kṛṣṇa's devotional service. Yet how little we understand this. Only a short time ago were our hearts full to the brim with unquenchable material desires. But instead you made our cups run over with the nectar of *kṛṣṇa-prema,* thus extinguishing the blazing fire of material existence. Yet how little we appreciate this! Only a short time ago our minds and intellects were totally absorbed in the unlimited speculations and interpretations of human asses braying their philosophies about like the new savior of the world. Yet with your empowered words you smashed such mental monsters and established Śrī Kṛṣṇa as the Absolute Truth and the summum bonum of existence within our hearts. Yet how little do we comprehend such an important achievement. And the list goes on, Śrīla Prabhupāda, of how you saved us from *māyā's den.* Yet we continuously find ourselves falling short of the mark, losing control of our senses and mind, lacking substantial inspiration, hardly living up to your highest expectations. We find ourselves constantly forgetting your divine character and our own constitutional position as eternal servants of Kṛṣṇa. And how many times have we found ourselves completely neglecting your orders and instructions, which are nondifferent from your very self? And how many days go by without our saying, from the bottom of our hearts, "Thank you, Śrīla Prabhupāda, thank you"? Finally, and most regrettably, Śrīla Prabhupāda, in this weakened condition, how can we ever hope to implement those words which you once wrote in your "Prayer to the Lotus Feet of Kṛṣṇa"?

"By the strong desire of Śrī Śrīmad Bhaktisiddhānta Sarasvatī Ṭhākura, the holy name of Lord Gaurāṅga will spread throughout all the countries of the Western world. In all the cities, towns, and villages of the earth, from all the oceans, seas, rivers, and streams, everyone will chant the holy name of Kṛṣṇa.

"As the vast mercy of Śrī Caitanya Mahāprabhu conquers all directions, a flood of transcendental ecstasy will certainly cover the land. When all the sinful, miserable living entities become happy, the Vaiṣṇavas' desire is then fulfilled."

O Prabhupāda, we long to see the day when the high court judges will adorn their faces with *tilaka,* when your books alone will remain as the lawbooks of mankind for thousands of years to come, and for the day when historians will describe your *saṅkīrtana* movement as the movement which saved the world in its darkest hour.

Therefore, on this day we pray with folded palms, Śrīla Prabhupāda, O master and lord, offering obeisances thousands of times in the dust of your unparalleled lotus feet, that we may somehow or other become instruments to fulfill your great, transcendental desire and see these wonderful, far-reaching words of yours fructify to their utmost import, changing the face of the entire world.

*Your humble servants at ISKCON Dallas,*
